/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v3 - SISTEMA CENTRAL DE OVERLAYS (Fase 2.3 & 2.4)
 * - Scrim preto 72% com fade de 200ms na entrada e 140ms na saída.
 * - Diálogos entram de escala 0.94 para 1.0 com mola líquida e fade, saem com fade de 140ms.
 * - BottomSheets sobem com spring(0.85f, 300f), fecham com arrasto vertical ou mola.
 * - PredictiveBackHandler nativo: escala (1 - 0.08p), raio (+28dp) e alpha (1 - 0.3p).
 * - Respeita rememberReduceMotion() e anima apenas na fase de graphicsLayer / desenho.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroOverlay(
    visible: Boolean = true,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    type: AuroOverlayType = AuroOverlayType.Dialog,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    var isVisible by remember { mutableStateOf(visible) }
    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val reduceMotion = rememberReduceMotion()

    // Sincroniza se o estado externo mudar
    LaunchedEffect(visible) {
        isVisible = visible
    }

    fun triggerDismiss() {
        if (reduceMotion) {
            onDismiss()
        } else {
            isVisible = false
            coroutineScope.launch {
                val dismissDelay = if (type == AuroOverlayType.BottomSheet) 220L else 140L
                delay(dismissDelay)
                onDismiss()
            }
        }
    }

    // Voltar preditivo (Fase 2.4)
    val backProgress = remember { Animatable(0f) }

    PredictiveBackHandler(enabled = isVisible) { progressFlow: Flow<BackEventCompat> ->
        try {
            progressFlow.collect { event ->
                if (!reduceMotion) {
                    backProgress.snapTo(event.progress)
                }
            }
            triggerDismiss()
        } catch (_: CancellationException) {
            if (!reduceMotion) {
                backProgress.animateTo(0f, spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f))
            }
        } finally {
            if (!reduceMotion) {
                backProgress.snapTo(0f)
            }
        }
    }

    Box(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentAlignment = if (type == AuroOverlayType.Dialog) Alignment.Center else Alignment.BottomCenter
    ) {
        // Scrim preto a 72% (fade 200ms entra, 140ms sai)
        AnimatedVisibility(
            visible = isVisible,
            enter = if (reduceMotion) EnterTransition.None else fadeIn(tween(200, easing = LinearEasing)),
            exit = if (reduceMotion) ExitTransition.None else fadeOut(tween(140, easing = LinearEasing))
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
                    .clickable(
                        interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                        indication = null,
                        onClick = { triggerDismiss() }
                    )
            )
        }

        // Painel do Overlay de acordo com o tipo
        if (type == AuroOverlayType.Dialog) {
            AnimatedVisibility(
                visible = isVisible,
                enter = if (reduceMotion) EnterTransition.None else {
                    scaleIn(
                        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f),
                        initialScale = 0.94f
                    ) + fadeIn(tween(200, easing = LinearEasing))
                },
                exit = if (reduceMotion) ExitTransition.None else {
                    scaleOut(
                        animationSpec = tween(140, easing = LinearEasing),
                        targetScale = 0.94f
                    ) + fadeOut(tween(140, easing = LinearEasing))
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.Center)
                    .graphicsLayer {
                        val p = backProgress.value
                        scaleX = 1f - 0.08f * p
                        scaleY = 1f - 0.08f * p
                        alpha = 1f - 0.3f * p
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape((AuroRadius.card.value + 28f * p).dp)
                        clip = p > 0f
                    }
                    .clickable(
                        interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                        indication = null,
                        onClick = { /* Bloqueia clique para não fechar o scrim */ }
                    )
            ) {
                content()
            }
        } else {
            val sheetDragOffset = remember { Animatable(0f) }

            AnimatedVisibility(
                visible = isVisible,
                enter = if (reduceMotion) EnterTransition.None else {
                    slideInVertically(
                        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f)
                    ) { it } + fadeIn(tween(200, easing = LinearEasing))
                },
                exit = if (reduceMotion) ExitTransition.None else {
                    slideOutVertically(
                        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f)
                    ) { it } + fadeOut(tween(140, easing = LinearEasing))
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                    .graphicsLayer {
                        val p = backProgress.value
                        scaleX = 1f - 0.08f * p
                        scaleY = 1f - 0.08f * p
                        alpha = 1f - 0.3f * p
                        translationY = sheetDragOffset.value
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(
                            topStart = (AuroRadius.card.value + 28f * p).dp,
                            topEnd = (AuroRadius.card.value + 28f * p).dp
                        )
                        clip = true
                    }
                    .pointerInput(reduceMotion) {
                        if (reduceMotion) return@pointerInput
                        detectVerticalDragGestures(
                            onDragEnd = {
                                if (sheetDragOffset.value > 140f) {
                                    triggerDismiss()
                                } else {
                                    coroutineScope.launch {
                                        sheetDragOffset.animateTo(0f, spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f))
                                    }
                                }
                            },
                            onDragCancel = {
                                coroutineScope.launch {
                                    sheetDragOffset.animateTo(0f, spring(dampingRatio = 0.85f, stiffness = 300f))
                                }
                            },
                            onVerticalDrag = { change, dragAmount ->
                                if (dragAmount > 0 || sheetDragOffset.value > 0) {
                                    change.consume()
                                    val newOffset = (sheetDragOffset.value + dragAmount).coerceAtLeast(0f)
                                    coroutineScope.launch {
                                        sheetDragOffset.snapTo(newOffset)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        )
                    }
                    .clickable(
                        interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                        indication = null,
                        onClick = { /* Bloqueia clique para não fechar o scrim */ }
                    )
            ) {
                content()
            }
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/ui/navigation/PlayerNav.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.ui.navigation

import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import com.lucas.player.model.LibraryCategory

/**
 * Destinos de navegação do Player inspirados na arquitetura do Poweramp (build 1034):
 * - Level 0: Now Playing (cmd_nav_to_main)
 * - Level 1: Library Root (cmd_nav_to_lib)
 * - Level 2: Category List (Pastas, Álbuns, Artistas, Gêneros, etc.)
 * - Level 3: Category Drilldown (Faixas de um Álbum, Álbuns de um Artista, etc.)
 * - Telas de utilidade: Equalizador (cmd_nav_to_eq), Busca (cmd_nav_to_search), Fila
 */
sealed class NavScreen {
    object NowPlaying : NavScreen()
    object LibraryRoot : NavScreen()
    data class CategoryList(val category: LibraryCategory) : NavScreen()
    data class Drilldown(
        val category: LibraryCategory,
        val title: String,
        val subtitle: String = ""
    ) : NavScreen()
    object Equalizer : NavScreen()
    object Search : NavScreen()
    object Queue : NavScreen()
    object Settings : NavScreen()
}

/**
 * Controlador de navegação com pilha de histórico (backstack) e comportamentos
 * idênticos aos observados na engenharia reversa do Poweramp.
 */
class PlayerNavController(initialScreen: NavScreen = NavScreen.LibraryRoot) {
    private val backStack = mutableStateListOf<NavScreen>(initialScreen)

    val currentScreen: NavScreen
        get() = backStack.lastOrNull() ?: NavScreen.LibraryRoot

    val canNavigateUp: Boolean
        get() = backStack.size > 1

    /**
     * Navega para uma nova tela, empilhando no histórico.
     * Evita duplicar a mesma tela no topo.
     */
    fun navigateTo(screen: NavScreen) {
        if (backStack.lastOrNull() != screen) {
            backStack.add(screen)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Volta um nível na hierarquia de navegação (Equivalente ao cmd_list_up_nav do Poweramp).
     * @return true se desempilhou, false se estava na raiz.
     */
    fun navigateUp(): Boolean {
        return if (backStack.size > 1) {
            backStack.removeAt(backStack.size - 1)
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    /**
     * Comportamento do botão "Biblioteca" na barra inferior do Poweramp:
     * - Se estiver no NowPlaying -> Volta para a última lista da biblioteca ou para a Raiz.
     * - Se estiver em um Drilldown -> Sobe para a lista da categoria.
     * - Se estiver na lista de categoria -> Sobe para a Raiz da biblioteca.
     * - Se já estiver na Raiz da biblioteca -> Alterna (toggle) de volta para o NowPlaying!
     */
    fun toggleLibrary() {
        when (val curr = currentScreen) {
            is NavScreen.NowPlaying -> {
                // Tenta achar a tela de biblioteca anterior no histórico
                val lastLibIndex = backStack.indexOfLast { it != NavScreen.NowPlaying && it != NavScreen.Equalizer && it != NavScreen.Search }
                if (lastLibIndex >= 0) {
                    // Mantém o histórico até lá
                    while (backStack.size > lastLibIndex + 1) {
                        backStack.removeAt(backStack.size - 1)
                    }
                } else {
                    navigateTo(NavScreen.LibraryRoot)
                }
            }
            is NavScreen.Drilldown -> {
                navigateTo(NavScreen.CategoryList(curr.category))
            }
            is NavScreen.CategoryList -> {
                navigateTo(NavScreen.LibraryRoot)
            }
            is NavScreen.LibraryRoot -> {
                navigateTo(NavScreen.NowPlaying)
            }
            else -> {
                navigateTo(NavScreen.LibraryRoot)
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Comportamento do botão "Equalizador" na barra inferior do Poweramp:
     * Toca no EQ -> Abre EQ; Toca de novo -> Retorna para a tela anterior.
     */
    fun toggleEqualizer() {
        if (currentScreen is NavScreen.Equalizer) {
            navigateUp()
        } else {
            navigateTo(NavScreen.Equalizer)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Comportamento do botão "Buscar" na barra inferior do Poweramp:
     */
    fun toggleSearch() {
        if (currentScreen is NavScreen.Search) {
            navigateUp()
        } else {
            navigateTo(NavScreen.Search)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Abre a tela do NowPlaying ao tocar em uma faixa ou no MiniPlayer.
     */
    fun openNowPlaying() {
        navigateTo(NavScreen.NowPlaying)
    }

    /**
     * Trata o botão Voltar físico/gestual do Android:
     * Retorna true se consumiu o evento de voltar, ou false se o app deve fechar.
     */
    fun handleBack(): Boolean {
        if (backStack.size > 1) {
            backStack.removeAt(backStack.size - 1)
            return true
        }
        return false
    }

    /**
     * Reseta a navegação diretamente para a Raiz da Biblioteca.
     */
    fun resetToLibraryRoot() {
        backStack.clear()
        backStack.add(NavScreen.LibraryRoot)
    }
}

import androidx.compose.foundation.background

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v2 - TELA RAIZ DA BIBLIOTECA (AURŌ LIBRARY ROOT)
 * 7.4: Dois atalhos de vidro (Aleatório e Continuar) e linhas de categoria de 50dp com contagens.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroLibraryRootScreen(
    totalTracksCount: Int,
    isLoading: Boolean = false,
    loadingCount: Int = 0,
    categoryCounts: Map<LibraryCategory, Int> = emptyMap(),
    onShuffleAll: () -> Unit = {},
    onResumePlayback: () -> Unit = {},
    onSelectCategory: (LibraryCategory) -> Unit,
    onSortClick: () -> Unit,
    onRescanClick: () -> Unit,
    listState: LazyListState = rememberLazyListState(),
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    var showLoadingBar by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showMenu by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    LaunchedEffect(isLoading) {
        if (isLoading) {
            delay(150)
            showLoadingBar = true
        } else {
            showLoadingBar = false
        }
    }

    val categories = remember {
        listOf(
            LibraryCategory.ALL_SONGS to Icons.Outlined.MusicNote,
            LibraryCategory.FOLDERS to Icons.Outlined.Folder,
            LibraryCategory.ALBUMS to Icons.Outlined.Album,
            LibraryCategory.ARTISTS to Icons.Outlined.Person,
            LibraryCategory.ALBUM_ARTISTS to Icons.Outlined.Group,
            LibraryCategory.GENRES to Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.Label,
            LibraryCategory.PLAYLISTS to Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.QueueMusic,
            LibraryCategory.QUEUE to Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.FormatListBulleted,
            LibraryCategory.YEARS to Icons.Outlined.CalendarToday,
            LibraryCategory.COMPOSERS to Icons.Outlined.Piano,
            LibraryCategory.MOST_PLAYED to Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.TrendingUp,
            LibraryCategory.RECENTLY_ADDED to Icons.Outlined.Schedule,
            LibraryCategory.RECENTLY_PLAYED to Icons.Outlined.History,
            LibraryCategory.TOP_RATED to Icons.Outlined.StarOutline
        )
    }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Black)
    ) {
        AuroTopBar(
            title = stringResource(R.string.library_breadcrumb),
            subtitle = pluralStringResource(R.plurals.tracks_indexed_count, totalTracksCount, totalTracksCount),
            isRoot = true,
            rightActions = {
                Box {
                    AuroGlass(
                        kind = AuroGlassKind.Button,
                        cornerRadius = 20.dp,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(AuroHeight.GlassButton),
                        onClick = {
                            AuroHaptics.click(view)
                            showMenu = true
                        }
                    ) {
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.MoreVert,
                                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_more_options),
                                tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    DropdownMenu(
                        expanded = showMenu,
                        onDismissRequest = { showMenu = false },
                        modifier = Modifier.background(AuroColor.Black)
                    ) {
                        DropdownMenuItem(
                            text = { Text(stringResource(R.string.cd_sort), style = AuroType.Body) },
                            onClick = {
                                showMenu = false
                                onSortClick()
                            }
                        )
                        DropdownMenuItem(
                            text = { Text(stringResource(R.string.cd_refresh), style = AuroType.Body) },
                            onClick = {
                                showMenu = false
                                onRescanClick()
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        )

        // Indicador de Carregamento com debounce de 150ms
        if (showLoadingBar) {
            AuroLoadingBar()
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.library_loading_count, if (loadingCount > 0) loadingCount else totalTracksCount),
                style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                    color = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                ),
                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 6.dp)
            )
        }

        if (totalTracksCount == 0 && !isLoading) {
            AuroEmptyState(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.MusicOff,
                title = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_title),
                message = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_message),
                actionLabel = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_action),
                onAction = onRescanClick,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(bottom = 0.dp)
            )
        } else {
            Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                LazyColumn(
                    state = listState,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = LocalAuroBottomInset.current + 24.dp)
                ) {
                    // Dois cards de vidro lado a lado: Aleatório e Continuar (154x64dp, raio 24)
                    item {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 10.dp),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                        ) {
                            AuroGlass(
                                kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                                cornerRadius = 24.dp,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .weight(1f)
                                    .height(64.dp),
                                onClick = {
                                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                                    onShuffleAll()
                                }
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Shuffle,
                                        contentDescription = null,
                                        tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = stringResource(R.string.shuffle_all),
                                        style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
                                    )
                                }
                            }

                            AuroGlass(
                                kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                                cornerRadius = 24.dp,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .weight(1f)
                                    .height(64.dp),
                                onClick = {
                                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                                    onResumePlayback()
                                }
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.PlayArrow,
                                        contentDescription = null,
                                        tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = stringResource(R.string.action_resume),
                                        style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Linhas de categoria (sem vidro, texto, contagem à direita e fio Hairline direto no preto)
                    items(categories, key = { it.first.name }) { (cat, icon) ->
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(AuroHeight.CategoryRow)
                                .clickable {
                                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                                    onSelectCategory(cat)
                                }
                                .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = icon,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(cat.getTitleRes()),
                                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            )
                            val count = categoryCounts[cat] ?: if (cat == LibraryCategory.ALL_SONGS) totalTracksCount else null
                            if (count != null && count > 0) {
                                Text(
                                    text = count.toString(),
                                    style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                        color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                                        fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }
                        HorizontalDivider(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 66.dp),
                            color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                            thickness = 1.dp
                        )
                    }
                }

                // Fade de 16dp no topo da lista
                AuroTopFade(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter))
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.lucas.player.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.ExperimentalFoundationApi

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v2 - TELA DE LISTA DE CATEGORIA (AURŌ CATEGORY LIST)
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun AuroCategoryListScreen(
    category: LibraryCategory,
    displayedTracks: List<Track>,
    displayedGroups: List<GroupItem>,
    currentPlayingId: Long,
    onBackClick: () -> Unit,
    onTrackClick: (Track, Int) -> Unit,
    onTrackLongClick: (Track) -> Unit,
    onTrackMenuClick: (Track) -> Unit,
    onGroupClick: (GroupItem) -> Unit,
    onGroupLongClick: (GroupItem) -> Unit = {},
    onPlayAll: () -> Unit = {},
    onShuffleAll: () -> Unit = {},
    onCreatePlaylist: () -> Unit = {},
    listState: androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyListState = rememberLazyListState(),
    onSortClick: () -> Unit,
    errorTrackIds: Set<Long> = emptySet(),
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    isSelectionMode: Boolean = false,
    selectedTrackIds: Set<Long> = emptySet(),
    onToggleSelectTrack: (Track) -> Unit = {},
    showFileFormat: Boolean = true
) {
    val isTrackListCategory = category == LibraryCategory.ALL_SONGS ||
            category == LibraryCategory.MOST_PLAYED ||
            category == LibraryCategory.RECENTLY_ADDED ||
            category == LibraryCategory.RECENTLY_PLAYED ||
            category == LibraryCategory.TOP_RATED
    val isPlaylists = category == LibraryCategory.PLAYLISTS

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Black)
    ) {
        AuroTopBar(
            title = stringResource(category.getTitleRes()),
            subtitle = if (isTrackListCategory) "${displayedTracks.size} faixas" else "${displayedGroups.size} itens",
            canGoBack = true,
            onBackClick = onBackClick,
            rightActions = {
                IconButton(onClick = onSortClick, modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp)) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.Sort,
                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_sort),
                        tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        )

        // Banner de Ações Rápidas em categorias baseadas em faixas
        if (isTrackListCategory && displayedTracks.isNotEmpty()) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 8.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                AuroGlass(
                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                    onClick = onPlayAll,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(42.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.PlayArrow,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.play_all),
                            style = AuroType.Label
                        )
                    }
                }

                AuroGlass(
                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                    onClick = onShuffleAll,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(42.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Shuffle,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.shuffle_all),
                            style = AuroType.Label
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
            HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
        }

        // Ação de Nova Lista de Reprodução
        if (isPlaylists) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clickable { onCreatePlaylist() }
                    .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Add,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.new_playlist_button),
                    style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                        color = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                )
            }
            HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
        }

        val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()

        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            if (isTrackListCategory) {
                if (displayedTracks.isEmpty()) {
                    AuroEmptyState(
                        icon = Icons.Rounded.MusicOff,
                        title = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_title),
                        message = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_message),
                        actionLabel = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_action),
                        onAction = onBackClick,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 0.dp)
                    )
                } else {
                    LazyColumn(
                        state = listState,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = LocalAuroBottomInset.current + 24.dp, end = if (displayedTracks.size > 15) 28.dp else 0.dp)
                    ) {
                        itemsIndexed(displayedTracks, key = { _, t -> t.id }) { index, track ->
                            AuroTrackRow(
                                track = track,
                                trackIndex = index,
                                isPlayingThis = track.id == currentPlayingId,
                                hasError = errorTrackIds.contains(track.id),
                                isSelectionMode = isSelectionMode,
                                isSelected = selectedTrackIds.contains(track.id),
                                showFormatTag = showFileFormat,
                                onClick = {
                                    if (isSelectionMode) {
                                        onToggleSelectTrack(track)
                                    } else {
                                        onTrackClick(track, index)
                                    }
                                },
                                onLongClick = { onTrackLongClick(track) },
                                onMenuClick = { onTrackMenuClick(track) }
                            )
                            HorizontalDivider(
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 80.dp),
                                color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                                thickness = 1.dp
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (displayedTracks.size > 15) {
                        AuroAlphabetFastScroller(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.CenterEnd)
                                .padding(bottom = 0.dp),
                            onLetterSelected = { letter ->
                                val targetIndex = if (letter == '#') {
                                    displayedTracks.indexOfFirst { it.title.firstOrNull()?.isLetter() == false }
                                } else {
                                    displayedTracks.indexOfFirst { matchesFirstLetter(it.title, letter) }
                                }
                                if (targetIndex >= 0) {
                                    coroutineScope.launch { listState.scrollToItem(targetIndex) }
                                }
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            } else {
                if (displayedGroups.isEmpty()) {
                    if (isPlaylists) {
                        AuroEmptyState(
                            icon = Icons.AutoMirrored.Rounded.QueueMusic,
                            title = stringResource(R.string.empty_playlist_title),
                            message = stringResource(R.string.empty_playlist_message),
                            actionLabel = stringResource(R.string.create_playlist),
                            onAction = onCreatePlaylist,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 0.dp)
                        )
                    } else {
                        AuroEmptyState(
                            icon = Icons.Rounded.FolderOff,
                            title = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_title),
                            message = stringResource(R.string.empty_library_message),
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 0.dp)
                        )
                    }
                } else {
                    LazyColumn(
                        state = listState,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = LocalAuroBottomInset.current + 24.dp, end = if (displayedGroups.size > 15) 28.dp else 0.dp)
                    ) {
                        items(displayedGroups, key = { it.title }) { group ->
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .combinedClickable(
                                        onClick = { onGroupClick(group) },
                                        onLongClick = { onGroupLongClick(group) }
                                    )
                                    .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 14.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = group.title,
                                        style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = group.subtitle,
                                        style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
                                    )
                                }
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Rounded.ChevronRight,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                )
                            }
                            HorizontalDivider(
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 66.dp),
                                color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                                thickness = 1.dp
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    if (displayedGroups.size > 15) {
                        AuroAlphabetFastScroller(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .align(Alignment.CenterEnd)
                                .padding(bottom = 0.dp),
                            onLetterSelected = { letter ->
                                val targetIndex = if (letter == '#') {
                                    displayedGroups.indexOfFirst { it.title.firstOrNull()?.isLetter() == false }
                                } else {
                                    displayedGroups.indexOfFirst { matchesFirstLetter(it.title, letter) }
                                }
                                if (targetIndex >= 0) {
                                    coroutineScope.launch { listState.scrollToItem(targetIndex) }
                                }
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // Fade sutil de 16dp no topo das listas
            AuroTopFade(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter))
        }
    }
}
