import androidx.compose.ui.focus.focusRequester
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.SolidColor
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalSoftwareKeyboardController
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalView
import androidx.compose.ui.res.pluralStringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontFamily
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.ImeAction
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextOverflow
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.lucas.player.R
import com.lucas.player.data.PreferencesManager
import com.lucas.player.model.Album
import com.lucas.player.model.Artist
import com.lucas.player.model.Track
import com.lucas.player.ui.components.AuroEmptyState
import com.lucas.player.ui.components.AuroHaptics
import com.lucas.player.ui.components.AuroTopFade
import com.lucas.player.ui.components.AuroTrackRow
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.AuroColor
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.AuroGlass
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.AuroGlassKind
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.AuroHeight
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.AuroSpace
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.AuroType
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.LocalAuroAccent
import com.lucas.player.ui.theme.LocalAuroBottomInset
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import java.util.Locale

enum class SearchFilter {
    ALL, TRACKS, ALBUMS, ARTISTS
}

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v2 - TELA DE BUSCA (AURŌ SEARCH - FASE 7.4)
 * - Campo: BasicTextField dentro de AuroGlass(Card) de 52dp (raio 26), sem TextField do Material.
 *   Ícone de lupa 24dp, dica em TextTertiary, X de limpar com toque 48dp, cursor e alças em accent A.
 *   Em foco, o reflexo sobe x1.4 (uSpec) com mola.
 * - Chips (32dp, contorno 1dp branco 18%. Selecionado: fundo 12%, contorno accent 70%, texto branco).
 * - Cabeçalho de seção: Caption em maiúsculas, TextTertiary, +1sp.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroSearchScreen(
    searchQuery: String,
    onQueryChange: (String) -> Unit,
    allTracks: List<Track>,
    currentPlayingId: Long,
    preferencesManager: PreferencesManager,
    onTrackClick: (Track, Int) -> Unit,
    onTrackMenuClick: (Track) -> Unit,
    onTrackLongClick: (Track) -> Unit = {},
    onAlbumClick: (String) -> Unit,
    onArtistClick: (String) -> Unit,
    onClose: () -> Unit,
    errorTrackIds: Set<Long> = emptySet(),
    listState: LazyListState = rememberLazyListState(),
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    var queryInput by remember { mutableStateOf(searchQuery) }
    var debouncedQuery by remember { mutableStateOf(searchQuery) }
    var selectedFilter by remember { mutableStateOf(SearchFilter.ALL) }
    val recentSearches = remember { mutableStateListOf<String>() }
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        recentSearches.clear()
        recentSearches.addAll(preferencesManager.getRecentSearches())
    }

    // Debounce de 250ms ao digitar
    LaunchedEffect(queryInput) {
        delay(250)
        debouncedQuery = queryInput
        onQueryChange(queryInput)
    }

    // Auto-ocultar teclado ao rolar
    val keyboardController = LocalSoftwareKeyboardController.current
    LaunchedEffect(listState.isScrollInProgress) {
        if (listState.isScrollInProgress) {
            keyboardController?.hide()
        }
    }

    val trimmed = debouncedQuery.trim()
    val hasQuery = trimmed.isNotEmpty()

    val matchedTracks = remember(trimmed, allTracks) {
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) emptyList()
        else allTracks.filter {
            it.title.contains(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) ||
            it.artist.contains(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) ||
            it.album.contains(trimmed, ignoreCase = true)
        }
    }

    val matchedAlbums = remember(trimmed, allTracks) {
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) emptyList()
        else {
            allTracks
                .filter { it.album.contains(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) }
                .groupBy { it.album }
                .map { (albumName, tracks) ->
                    val firstTrack = tracks.first()
                    Album(
                        name = albumName,
                        artist = firstTrack.artist,
                        year = firstTrack.year,
                        trackCount = tracks.size
                    )
                }
        }
    }

    val matchedArtists = remember(trimmed, allTracks) {
        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) emptyList()
        else {
            allTracks
                .filter { it.artist.contains(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) }
                .groupBy { it.artist }
                .map { (artistName, tracks) ->
                    Artist(
                        name = artistName,
                        trackCount = tracks.size
                    )
                }
        }
    }

    val hasAnyResults = matchedTracks.isNotEmpty() || matchedAlbums.isNotEmpty() || matchedArtists.isNotEmpty()

    val searchInteractionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val isSearchFocused by searchInteractionSource.collectIsFocusedAsState()
    val specMultiplier by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isSearchFocused) 1.4f else 1.0f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.8f, stiffness = Spring.StiffnessMediumLow),
        label = "search_spec_spring"
    )
    val focusRequester = remember { FocusRequester() }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Black)
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .imePadding()
            .padding(top = 12.dp)
    ) {
        // Campo de busca em vidro (52dp, raio 26dp)
        AuroGlass(
            kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
            cornerRadius = 26.dp,
            specMultiplier = specMultiplier,
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(AuroHeight.SearchField)
                .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin)
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Search,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))

                CompositionLocalProvider(
                    LocalTextSelectionColors provides TextSelectionColors(
                        handleColor = accent,
                        backgroundColor = accent.copy(alpha = 0.35f)
                    )
                ) {
                    BasicTextField(
                        value = queryInput,
                        onValueChange = { queryInput = it },
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1f)
                            .focusRequester(focusRequester),
                        textStyle = AuroType.Body.copy(color = AuroColor.TextPrimary),
                        cursorBrush = SolidColor(accent),
                        singleLine = true,
                        interactionSource = searchInteractionSource,
                        keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(imeAction = ImeAction.Search),
                        keyboardActions = KeyboardActions(
                            onSearch = {
                                keyboardController?.hide()
                                if (queryInput.isNotBlank()) {
                                    preferencesManager.addRecentSearch(queryInput)
                                    recentSearches.clear()
                                    recentSearches.addAll(preferencesManager.getRecentSearches())
                                }
                            }
                        ),
                        decorationBox = { innerTextField ->
                            Box(contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart) {
                                if (queryInput.isEmpty()) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = stringResource(R.string.search_placeholder),
                                        style = AuroType.Body.copy(color = AuroColor.TextTertiary)
                                    )
                                }
                                innerTextField()
                            }
                        }
                    )
                }

                if (queryInput.isNotEmpty()) {
                    IconButton(
                        onClick = {
                            queryInput = ""
                            debouncedQuery = ""
                            onQueryChange("")
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.size(AuroHeight.TouchTarget)
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Close,
                            contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_clear_search),
                            tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))

        // Chips de Filtro (32dp, 1dp contorno branco 18%. Selecionado: fundo 12%, contorno accent 70%, texto branco)
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 6.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            SearchFilterChip(
                label = stringResource(R.string.search_filter_all),
                isSelected = selectedFilter == SearchFilter.ALL,
                onClick = { selectedFilter = SearchFilter.ALL }
            )
            SearchFilterChip(
                label = stringResource(R.string.search_filter_tracks),
                isSelected = selectedFilter == SearchFilter.TRACKS,
                onClick = { selectedFilter = SearchFilter.TRACKS }
            )
            SearchFilterChip(
                label = stringResource(R.string.search_filter_albums),
                isSelected = selectedFilter == SearchFilter.ALBUMS,
                onClick = { selectedFilter = SearchFilter.ALBUMS }
            )
            SearchFilterChip(
                label = stringResource(R.string.search_filter_artists),
                isSelected = selectedFilter == SearchFilter.ARTISTS,
                onClick = { selectedFilter = SearchFilter.ARTISTS }
            )
        }

        // Conteúdo: Buscas Recentes OU Resultados
        if (!hasQuery) {
            // Buscas Recentes
            if (recentSearches.isNotEmpty()) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 10.dp),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.search_recent_title).uppercase(Locale.getDefault()),
                        style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                            color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            letterSpacing = 1.sp
                        )
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.search_recent_clear),
                        style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextTertiary),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clickable {
                                preferencesManager.clearRecentSearches()
                                recentSearches.clear()
                            }
                            .padding(4.dp)
                    )
                }

                Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    LazyColumn(
                        state = listState,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = LocalAuroBottomInset.current + 24.dp)
                    ) {
                        itemsIndexed(recentSearches, key = { _, item -> item }) { _, queryItem ->
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .clickable {
                                        queryInput = queryItem
                                        debouncedQuery = queryItem
                                        keyboardController?.hide()
                                    }
                                    .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Outlined.History,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = queryItem,
                                    style = AuroType.Body,
                                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                )
                                IconButton(
                                    onClick = {
                                        preferencesManager.removeRecentSearch(queryItem)
                                        recentSearches.remove(queryItem)
                                    },
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(AuroHeight.TouchTarget)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Close,
                                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.delete),
                                        tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            HorizontalDivider(
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 54.dp),
                                color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                                thickness = 1.dp
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    AuroTopFade(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter))
                }
            }
        } else if (!hasAnyResults) {
            // Estado Vazio de Busca
            AuroEmptyState(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.SearchOff,
                title = stringResource(R.string.empty_search_title, trimmed),
                message = stringResource(R.string.empty_search_message),
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(bottom = 0.dp)
            )
        } else {
            // Lista de Resultados
            Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                LazyColumn(
                    state = listState,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = LocalAuroBottomInset.current + 24.dp)
                ) {
                    when (selectedFilter) {
                        SearchFilter.ALL -> {
                            // Grupo: Faixas (máximo 5 + Ver todos)
                            if (matchedTracks.isNotEmpty()) {
                                item {
                                    SearchSectionHeader(
                                        title = stringResource(R.string.search_section_tracks),
                                        canViewAll = matchedTracks.size > 5,
                                        onViewAll = { selectedFilter = SearchFilter.TRACKS }
                                    )
                                }
                                itemsIndexed(matchedTracks.take(5), key = { _, t -> "all_t_${t.id}" }) { index, track ->
                                    AuroTrackRow(
                                        track = track,
                                        trackIndex = index,
                                        isPlayingThis = track.id == currentPlayingId,
                                        hasError = errorTrackIds.contains(track.id),
                                        onClick = {
                                            preferencesManager.addRecentSearch(trimmed)
                                            keyboardController?.hide()
                                            onTrackClick(track, index)
                                        },
                                        onLongClick = { onTrackLongClick(track) },
                                        onMenuClick = { onTrackMenuClick(track) }
                                    )
                                    HorizontalDivider(
                                        modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 80.dp),
                                        color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                                        thickness = 1.dp
                                    )
                                }
                            }

                            // Grupo: Álbuns (máximo 5 + Ver todos)
                            if (matchedAlbums.isNotEmpty()) {
                                item {
                                    SearchSectionHeader(
                                        title = stringResource(R.string.search_section_albums),
                                        canViewAll = matchedAlbums.size > 5,
                                        onViewAll = { selectedFilter = SearchFilter.ALBUMS }
                                    )
                                }
                                itemsIndexed(matchedAlbums.take(5), key = { _, a -> "all_a_${a.name}" }) { _, album ->
                                    SearchAlbumRow(
                                        album = album,
                                        onClick = {
                                            preferencesManager.addRecentSearch(trimmed)
                                            keyboardController?.hide()
                                            onAlbumClick(album.name)
                                        }
                                    )
                                    HorizontalDivider(
                                        modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 80.dp),
                                        color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                                        thickness = 1.dp
                                    )
                                }
                            }

                            // Grupo: Artistas (máximo 5 + Ver todos)
                            if (matchedArtists.isNotEmpty()) {
                                item {
                                    SearchSectionHeader(
                                        title = stringResource(R.string.search_section_artists),
                                        canViewAll = matchedArtists.size > 5,
                                        onViewAll = { selectedFilter = SearchFilter.ARTISTS }
                                    )
                                }
                                itemsIndexed(matchedArtists.take(5), key = { _, ar -> "all_ar_${ar.name}" }) { _, artist ->
                                    SearchArtistRow(
                                        artist = artist,
                                        onClick = {
                                            preferencesManager.addRecentSearch(trimmed)
                                            keyboardController?.hide()
                                            onArtistClick(artist.name)
                                        }
                                    )
                                    HorizontalDivider(
                                        modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 80.dp),
                                        color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                                        thickness = 1.dp
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        SearchFilter.TRACKS -> {
                            itemsIndexed(matchedTracks, key = { _, t -> "tracks_${t.id}" }) { index, track ->
                                AuroTrackRow(
                                    track = track,
                                    trackIndex = index,
                                    isPlayingThis = track.id == currentPlayingId,
                                    hasError = errorTrackIds.contains(track.id),
                                    onClick = {
                                        preferencesManager.addRecentSearch(trimmed)
                                        keyboardController?.hide()
                                        onTrackClick(track, index)
                                    },
                                    onLongClick = { onTrackLongClick(track) },
                                    onMenuClick = { onTrackMenuClick(track) }
                                )
                                HorizontalDivider(
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 80.dp),
                                    color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                                    thickness = 1.dp
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        SearchFilter.ALBUMS -> {
                            itemsIndexed(matchedAlbums, key = { _, a -> "albums_${a.name}" }) { _, album ->
                                SearchAlbumRow(
                                    album = album,
                                    onClick = {
                                        preferencesManager.addRecentSearch(trimmed)
                                        keyboardController?.hide()
                                        onAlbumClick(album.name)
                                    }
                                )
                                HorizontalDivider(
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 80.dp),
                                    color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                                    thickness = 1.dp
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        SearchFilter.ARTISTS -> {
                            itemsIndexed(matchedArtists, key = { _, ar -> "artists_${ar.name}" }) { _, artist ->
                                SearchArtistRow(
                                    artist = artist,
                                    onClick = {
                                        preferencesManager.addRecentSearch(trimmed)
                                        keyboardController?.hide()
                                        onArtistClick(artist.name)
                                    }
                                )
                                HorizontalDivider(
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 80.dp),
                                    color = AuroColor.Hairline,
                                    thickness = 1.dp
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                AuroTopFade(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter))
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SearchFilterChip(
    label: String,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value
    val view = LocalView.current
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .defaultMinSize(minHeight = AuroHeight.TouchTarget)
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                indication = null
            ) {
                AuroHaptics.tick(view)
                onClick()
            },
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .height(AuroHeight.Chip)
                .clip(shape)
                .background(if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f) else Color.Transparent)
                .border(
                    width = 1.dp,
                    color = if (isSelected) accent.copy(alpha = 0.70f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                    shape = shape
                )
                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Text(
                text = label,
                style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                    color = if (isSelected) AuroColor.TextPrimary else AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                )
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SearchSectionHeader(
    title: String,
    canViewAll: Boolean = false,
    onViewAll: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 10.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = title.uppercase(Locale.getDefault()),
            style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                letterSpacing = 1.sp
            )
        )
        if (canViewAll) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.search_view_all),
                style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = accent, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clickable(onClick = onViewAll)
                    .padding(4.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SearchAlbumRow(
    album: Album,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Album,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp))
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = album.name,
                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = "${album.artist} • ${pluralStringResource(R.plurals.tracks_count, album.trackCount, album.trackCount)}",
                style = AuroType.Caption.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary),
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
        }
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.ChevronRight,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SearchArtistRow(
    artist: Artist,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Person,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp))
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = artist.name,
                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = pluralStringResource(R.plurals.tracks_count, artist.trackCount, artist.trackCount),
                style = AuroType.Caption.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary),
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
        }
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.ChevronRight,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
        )
    }
}


=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/ui/screens/AuroEqualizerScreen.kt ===
package com.lucas.player.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.Canvas
import androidx.compose.foundation.ExperimentalFoundationApi
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.MutableInteractionSource
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyListState
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyRow
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.rememberLazyListState
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape

import kotlin.math.roundToInt

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v2 - TELA DE EQUALIZADOR (AURŌ EQUALIZER)
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun AuroEqualizerScreen(
    eqEnabled: Boolean,
    onToggleEq: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    toneEnabled: Boolean,
    onToggleTone: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    preampDb: Float,
    onPreampChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    bassDb: Float,
    onBassChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    trebleDb: Float,
    onTrebleChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    balance: Float,
    onBalanceChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    isMono: Boolean,
    onToggleMono: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    bandGains: List<Float>,
    onBandGainChange: (Int, Float) -> Unit,
    activePresetName: String,
    presets: List<MusicDatabase.EqPreset>,
    onSelectPreset: (MusicDatabase.EqPreset) -> Unit,
    onResetEq: () -> Unit = {},
    onSavePreset: () -> Unit = {},
    onDeletePreset: (MusicDatabase.EqPreset) -> Unit = {},
    onClose: () -> Unit,
    listState: LazyListState = rememberLazyListState(),
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value
    val bandFreqs = remember {
        listOf("31Hz", "62Hz", "125Hz", "250Hz", "500Hz", "1kHz", "2kHz", "4kHz", "8kHz", "16kHz")
    }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Black)
    ) {
        AuroTopBar(
            title = stringResource(R.string.equalizer_breadcrumb),
            canGoBack = true,
            onBackClick = onClose,
            rightActions = {
                AuroSwitch(
                    checked = eqEnabled,
                    onCheckedChange = onToggleEq
                )
            }
        )

        Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            LazyColumn(
                state = listState,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 12.dp, bottom = LocalAuroBottomInset.current + 24.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
            ) {
            // Presets
            item {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.eq_presets_header),
                        style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                            color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                            fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
                        ),
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    )
                    AuroGlass(
                        kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                        cornerRadius = 20.dp,
                        onClick = onResetEq,
                        modifier = Modifier.height(AuroHeight.GlassButton)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp)
                                .fillMaxHeight(),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.RestartAlt,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.eq_reset),
                                style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    AuroGlass(
                        kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                        cornerRadius = 20.dp,
                        onClick = onSavePreset,
                        modifier = Modifier.height(AuroHeight.GlassButton)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp)
                                .fillMaxHeight(),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.BookmarkAdd,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = accent,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.eq_save),
                                style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                                    color = accent,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                LazyRow(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    items(presets, key = { it.id }) { p ->
                        val isSelected = p.name.equals(activePresetName, ignoreCase = true)
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .height(32.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                .background(if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f) else Color.Transparent)
                                .border(
                                    width = 1.dp,
                                    color = if (isSelected) accent.copy(alpha = 0.70f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                                )
                                .clickable { onSelectPreset(p) }
                                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = p.name,
                                style = AuroType.Body.copy(
                                    color = if (isSelected) Color.White else AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                    fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Normal
                                )
                            )
                            if (!p.isBuiltin) {
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(20.dp)
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                        .clickable { onDeletePreset(p) },
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Close,
                                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_delete_preset),
                                        tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Tom & Pré-amp
            item {
                AuroGlass(
                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Text(
                                    text = stringResource(R.string.eq_tone_preamp_header),
                                    style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                        color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
                                    )
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                                AuroSwitch(
                                    checked = toneEnabled,
                                    onCheckedChange = onToggleTone,
                                    enabled = eqEnabled
                                )
                            }
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Text(
                                    text = stringResource(R.string.eq_mono),
                                    style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Checkbox(
                                    checked = isMono,
                                    onCheckedChange = onToggleMono,
                                    colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = accent)
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        AuroToneRow(label = stringResource(R.string.eq_preamp_label), value = preampDb, range = -12f..12f, enabled = eqEnabled, onValueChange = onPreampChange)
                        AuroToneRow(label = stringResource(R.string.eq_bass_label), value = bassDb, range = -12f..12f, enabled = eqEnabled && toneEnabled, onValueChange = onBassChange)
                        AuroToneRow(label = stringResource(R.string.eq_treble_label), value = trebleDb, range = -12f..12f, enabled = eqEnabled && toneEnabled, onValueChange = onTrebleChange)
                        AuroToneRow(label = stringResource(R.string.eq_balance_label), value = balance, range = -1f..1f, isBalance = true, enabled = true, onValueChange = onBalanceChange)
                    }
                }
            }

            // 10 Bandas
            item {
                AuroGlass(
                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.eq_bands_header),
                            style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
                            )
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                        // Curva de resposta gráfica de 2dp interpolada das 10 bandas
                        Canvas(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(48.dp)
                                .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                        ) {
                            val w = size.width
                            val h = size.height
                            val midY = h / 2f

                            // Linha de referência central 0 dB
                            drawLine(
                                color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.10f),
                                start = androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset(0f, midY),
                                end = androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset(w, midY),
                                strokeWidth = 1.dp.toPx()
                            )

                            if (bandGains.isNotEmpty()) {
                                val path = Path()
                                val stepX = if (bandGains.size > 1) w / (bandGains.size - 1) else w

                                val points = bandGains.mapIndexed { index, gain ->
                                    val clamped = gain.coerceIn(-12f, 12f)
                                    val x = index * stepX
                                    val y = midY - (clamped / 12f) * (h * 0.40f)
                                    androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset(x, y)
                                }

                                path.moveTo(points.first().x, points.first().y)
                                for (i in 0 until points.size - 1) {
                                    val p0 = points[i]
                                    val p1 = points[i + 1]
                                    val cx = (p0.x + p1.x) / 2f
                                    path.cubicTo(cx, p0.y, cx, p1.y, p1.x, p1.y)
                                }

                                drawPath(
                                    path = path,
                                    color = accent,
                                    style = Stroke(
                                        width = 2.dp.toPx(),
                                        cap = StrokeCap.Round,
                                        join = StrokeJoin.Round
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))

                        bandFreqs.forEachIndexed { bandIndex, freqLabel ->
                            val gain = bandGains.getOrElse(bandIndex) { 0f }
                            val formattedGain = String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%+.1f dB", gain)
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(vertical = 2.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = freqLabel,
                                    style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                        color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
                                    ),
                                    modifier = Modifier.width(50.dp)
                                )

                                AuroSlider(
                                    value = gain,
                                    onValueChange = { onBandGainChange(bandIndex, it) },
                                    valueRange = -12f..12f,
                                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_eq_band, freqLabel),
                                    stateDescription = formattedGain,
                                    enabled = eqEnabled,
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .height(36.dp)
                                )

                                Text(
                                    text = formattedGain,
                                    style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                        color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                                        fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
                                    ),
                                    textAlign = TextAlign.End,
                                    modifier = Modifier.width(54.dp)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Fade sutil de 16dp no topo
        AuroTopFade(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter))
    }
}
}

@Composable
private fun AuroToneRow(
    label: String,
    value: Float,
    range: ClosedFloatingPointRange<Float>,
    isBalance: Boolean = false,
    enabled: Boolean = true,
    onValueChange: (Float) -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(vertical = 2.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                color = if (enabled) AuroColor.TextSecondary else AuroColor.TextTertiary
            ),
            modifier = Modifier.width(86.dp)
        )
        val textVal = if (isBalance) {
            when {
                value < -0.05f -> "L ${(value * -100).roundToInt()}%"
                value > 0.05f -> "R ${(value * 100).roundToInt()}%"
                else -> stringResource(R.string.eq_balance_center)
            }
        } else {
            String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%+.1f dB", value)
        }
        AuroSlider(
            value = value,
            onValueChange = onValueChange,
            valueRange = range,
            contentDescription = label,
            stateDescription = textVal,
            enabled = enabled,
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .height(36.dp)
        )
        Text(
            text = textVal,
            style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                color = if (enabled) AuroColor.TextPrimary else AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
            ),
            textAlign = TextAlign.End,
            modifier = Modifier.width(54.dp)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v3 - TELA DE CONFIGURAÇÕES (AURŌ SETTINGS SCREEN)
 * Fase 4.2: Configurações persistidas no PreferencesManager, alinhadas às diretrizes do plano:
 * - Cabeçalhos em Label maiúsculo com Accent A
 * - Linhas mínimas de 56dp com divisor Hairline
 * - AuroSwitch (48x28dp) e AuroSlider com semantics
 * - Seções: Biblioteca, Reprodução, Aparência (com botão de vidro de exemplo ao vivo) e Sobre
 */
@Composable
fun AuroSettingsScreen(
    preferencesManager: PreferencesManager,
    onBackClick: () -> Unit,
    onRescanLibrary: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onSettingsChanged: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value

    // Estados Reativos de Preferências
    var pauseOnDisconnect by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isPauseOnHeadsetDisconnectEnabled()) }
    var gapless by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isGaplessEnabled()) }
    var crossfadeSeconds by remember { mutableIntStateOf(preferencesManager.getCrossfadeDurationMs() / 1000) }
    var replayGainMode by remember { mutableIntStateOf(preferencesManager.getReplayGainMode()) }
    var rgPreampWith by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(preferencesManager.getReplayGainPreampWithRg()) }
    var rgPreampWithout by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(preferencesManager.getReplayGainPreampWithoutRg()) }
    var resumePlayback by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isResumePlaybackEnabled()) }

    var tiltEnabled by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isLightReactionTiltEnabled()) }
    var reflectionSoftness by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(preferencesManager.getReflectionSoftness()) }
    var reduceMotionMode by remember { mutableIntStateOf(preferencesManager.getReduceMotionMode()) }
    var showFileFormat by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isShowFileFormatEnabled()) }

    var ignoreShorterThanSec by remember { mutableIntStateOf(preferencesManager.getIgnoreShorterThanSeconds()) }

    // Diálogos de Seleção
    var showReplayGainPicker by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showReduceMotionPicker by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showLicensesDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Black)
    ) {
        AuroTopBar(
            title = stringResource(R.string.settings_title),
            canGoBack = true,
            onBackClick = onBackClick
        )

        Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = LocalAuroBottomInset.current + 24.dp)
            ) {
            // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            // SEÇÃO 1: BIBLIOTECA
            // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            item {
                SettingsSectionHeader(stringResource(R.string.settings_section_library))
            }

            item {
                SettingsClickableRow(
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_music_folders),
                    subtitle = stringResource(R.string.settings_music_folders_desc),
                    onClick = { onRescanLibrary() },
                    trailingContent = {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.FolderOpen,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                    }
                )
                HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
            }

            item {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.settings_ignore_shorter_than),
                            style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = if (ignoreShorterThanSec == 0) {
                                stringResource(R.string.settings_crossfade_off)
                            } else {
                                stringResource(R.string.settings_ignore_shorter_than_sec, ignoreShorterThanSec)
                            },
                            style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                                color = accent,
                                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                            )
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    AuroSlider(
                        value = ignoreShorterThanSec.toFloat(),
                        onValueChange = {
                            val rounded = it.roundToInt()
                            ignoreShorterThanSec = rounded
                            preferencesManager.setIgnoreShorterThanSeconds(rounded)
                            onSettingsChanged()
                        },
                        valueRange = 0f..60f,
                        steps = 11
                    )
                }
                HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
            }

            item {
                SettingsClickableRow(
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_rescan_library),
                    subtitle = stringResource(R.string.settings_rescan_library_desc),
                    onClick = {
                        onRescanLibrary()
                    },
                    trailingContent = {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Refresh,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = accent,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                        )
                    }
                )
                HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
            }

            // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            // SEÇÃO 2: REPRODUÇÃO

            }

            item {

            item {

                        gapless = it
                        preferencesManager.setGaplessEnabled(it)
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
                HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
            }

            item {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.settings_crossfade),

                            onSettingsChanged()
                        },
                        valueRange = 0f..12f,
                        steps = 11
                    )
                }
                HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
            }

            item {
                val rgLabel = when (replayGainMode) {
                    1 -> stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain_track)
                    2 -> stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain_album)
                    else -> stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain_off)
                }
                SettingsClickableRow(
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain),
                    subtitle = rgLabel,
                    onClick = { showReplayGainPicker = true },
                    trailingContent = {
                        Icon(

                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                    }
                )
                HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
            }

            if (replayGainMode != 0) {
                item {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()

                        resumePlayback = it

                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_tilt_light),
                    subtitle = stringResource(R.string.settings_tilt_light_desc),

                        onSettingsChanged()

                                text = stringResource(R.string.settings_reflection_softness),

                        valueRange = 0f..1f,
                        steps = 20

                            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                    ) {

            item {

            }

            item {

                    subtitle = "v${BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME} (build ${BuildConfig.VERSION_CODE})"

            item {

            }

            item {
                SettingsClickableRow(
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_repository),
                    subtitle = "GitHub • Aurō Audio Engine v2.0",
                    onClick = {
                        val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("https://github.com/Lusqta/Player"))
                        try {
                            context.startActivity(intent)
                        } catch (_: Exception) {}
                    },
                    trailingContent = {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.OpenInNew,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                    }
                )
                HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
            }
        }

        // Fade sutil de 16dp no topo
        AuroTopFade(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter))
    }
}

    // Modal Sheet de Escolha do ReplayGain
    if (showReplayGainPicker) {
        AuroOverlay(
            visible = true,
            onDismiss = { showReplayGainPicker = false },
            type = AuroOverlayType.BottomSheet
        ) {
            AuroSheetContainer(
                title = stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain),
                onDismiss = { showReplayGainPicker = false }
            ) {
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (replayGainMode == 0) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain_off),
                    onClick = {
                        replayGainMode = 0
                        preferencesManager.saveReplayGainMode(0)
                        showReplayGainPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (replayGainMode == 1) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain_track),
                    onClick = {
                        replayGainMode = 1
                        preferencesManager.saveReplayGainMode(1)
                        showReplayGainPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (replayGainMode == 2) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain_album),
                    onClick = {
                        replayGainMode = 2
                        preferencesManager.saveReplayGainMode(2)
                        showReplayGainPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
            }
        }
    }

    // Modal Sheet de Escolha de Animações
    if (showReduceMotionPicker) {
        AuroOverlay(
            visible = true,
            onDismiss = { showReduceMotionPicker = false },
            type = AuroOverlayType.BottomSheet
        ) {
            AuroSheetContainer(
                title = stringResource(R.string.settings_reduce_motion),
                onDismiss = { showReduceMotionPicker = false }
            ) {
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (reduceMotionMode == 0) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_reduce_motion_system),
                    onClick = {
                        reduceMotionMode = 0
                        preferencesManager.setReduceMotionMode(0)
                        showReduceMotionPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (reduceMotionMode == 1) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_reduce_motion_always),
                    onClick = {
                        reduceMotionMode = 1
                        preferencesManager.setReduceMotionMode(1)
                        showReduceMotionPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (reduceMotionMode == 2) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_reduce_motion_never),
                    onClick = {
                        reduceMotionMode = 2
                        preferencesManager.setReduceMotionMode(2)
                        showReduceMotionPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
            }
        }
    }

    // Diálogo de Licenças
    if (showLicensesDialog) {
        AuroOverlay(
            visible = true,
            onDismiss = { showLicensesDialog = false },
            type = AuroOverlayType.Dialog
        ) {
            AuroDialogContainer(
                title = stringResource(R.string.settings_licenses),
                onDismiss = { showLicensesDialog = false },
                actions = {
                    AuroDialogTextButton(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.close),
                        onClick = { showLicensesDialog = false }
                    )
                }
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.settings_licenses_text),
                    style = AuroType.Body.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsSectionHeader(title: String) {
    Text(
        text = title,
        style = AuroType.SectionHeader.copy(
            color = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
        ),
        modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp, top = 20.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsSwitchRow(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    checked: Boolean,
    onCheckedChange: (Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .defaultMinSize(minHeight = 56.dp)
            .clickable { onCheckedChange(!checked) }
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 16.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = subtitle,
                style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
            )
        }
        AuroSwitch(
            checked = checked,
            onCheckedChange = onCheckedChange
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsClickableRow(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    onClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    trailingContent: (@Composable () -> Unit)? = null
) {
    val clickMod = if (onClick != null) Modifier.clickable(onClick = onClick) else Modifier

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .defaultMinSize(minHeight = 56.dp)
            .then(clickMod)
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 12.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = subtitle,
                style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
            )
        }
        trailingContent?.invoke()
    }
}
